The user's message seems to be mostly Git log output from VS Code, but at the very end there's a question in Portuguese: "aonde posso fazer o login ?" which means "Where can I do the login?" or "Where can I log in?"

Given the context of the Ativo Real project, which has a login/authentication system with different user profiles (Topógrafo, Proprietário, Agricultor), the user is asking where they can access the login functionality.

Looking at the project structure from the copilot instructions:
- The project has a TechLoginForm.tsx for surveyor authentication
- There's a LoginPage.tsx in src/pages/
- The App.tsx manages different views and user profiles
- The landing page allows profile selection

The user likely wants to know where the login interface is located in the code or how to access it in the running application.

Let me provide a clear answer about the login functionality in the Ativo Real project.
Vejo que sua pergunta é em português: **"Aonde posso fazer o login?"** (Onde posso fazer login?)

No projeto **Ativo Real**, o login funciona assim:

## 🔐 Locais de Login

### 1. **Interface Principal** (Frontend)
- **URL**: `http://localhost:5173` (quando está rodando com `npm run dev`)
- **Arquivo**:
- **Fluxo**:
 1. Você vê a **landing page** com seleção de perfil
 2. Clica em um dos 3 perfis:
 - **Topógrafo** (Surveyor)
 - **Proprietário** (Owner)
 - **Agricultor** (Farmer)
 3. Dependendo do perfil, você é direcionado para o formulário de login apropriado

### 2. **Formulários de Login por Perfil**

| Perfil | Arquivo | Endpoint |
|--------|---------|----------|
| **Topógrafo** |
file:///c%3A/Users/User/progresso/ativo-real/src/components/Forms/TechLoginForm.tsx
| `POST /api/urban/activate` |

### 3. **Serviço de Autenticação** (Backend)
- **Arquivo**:
- Todos os logins passam por aqui via função `postJson()`

## 🚀 Como Acessar Agora

```bash
# Terminal 1: Frontend
cd c:\Users\User\progresso\ativo-real
npm run dev
# Acesse: http://localhost:5173

# Terminal 2: Backend
cd api
python -m venv .venv
.\.venv\Scripts\activate
pip install -r requirements.txt
func start
# Backend em: http://localhost:7071/api
```

**Qual perfil você quer acessar?** Posso ajudar com o fluxo de login específico!

Vantagens:

- Pronto para análise com pandas
- Fácil de parsear linha por linha
- Compacto e eficiente
```
**Opção B: SQLite - Para queries complexas**
```
Arquivo: .model_lab/monitoring/progress.db
Vantagens:
- Índices em steps/epochs
- Queries SQL (ex: max loss per epoch)
- Menor I/O (1 arquivo)
```
**Opção C: CSV - Simples & universal**
```
Arquivo: .model_lab/monitoring/progress.csv
Vantagens:
- Compatível com Excel/Sheets
- Leitura rápida com pandas
- Visualização imediata em ferramentas BI
```
**Recomendação**: Use **Opção A (JSONL)** + CSV export periódico
- JSONL para coleta em tempo real
- CSV diário para BI/dashboards
### 2.3 Estrutura do Repositório de Monitoramento
```
.model_lab/
├── monitoring/
│ ├── progress.jsonl # Histórico completo de checkpoints
│ ├── latest_checkpoint.json # Snapshot atual (último checkpoint)
│ ├── training_summary.json # Resumo executivo
│ ├── exports/
│ │ ├── progress_20250128.csv # Export diário
│ │ └── progress_20250127.csv
│ └── logs/
│ ├── training_20250128_143022.log # Logs textuais por run
│ └── errors_20250128.log # Apenas erros
├── checkpoints/ # Modelos salvos
│ ├── lora/
│ │ └── phi-silica-lora-20250128-143022/
│ │ ├── model.safetensors
│ │ ├── adapter_config.json
│ │ └── metadata.json
│ └── soft_prompt/
├── dataset/
│ ├── train.jsonl
│ ├── test.jsonl
│ └── dataset_stats.json
└── config/
├── monitoring.config.json # Config de monitoring
└── alert_thresholds.json # Limiares de alerta
```

## 3. INTEGRAÇÃO COM AUTO-SAVE (15 MINUTOS)
### 3.1 Linguagem Recomendada: **Python** (nativa no stack)
**Motivos:**
- Compatível com Azure Functions v2 (backend atual)
- Acesso nativo ao contexto do treinamento via scheduler
- Bibliotecas: `schedule`, `psutil`, `pandas`
- Windows/Linux/macOS compatível
### 3.2 Implementação: Auto-Save Monitor
**Arquivo: `.model_lab/scripts/autosave_monitor.py`**
```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Auto-save monitor para fine-tuning Phi-Silica 3.6
Coleta metrics a cada 15 min e salva em progress.jsonl
"""
import json
import os
import sys
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Optional, Dict, Any
import time
import subprocess
import psutil
import logging
# Configuração de logging
logging.basicConfig(
level=logging.INFO,
format='%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(message)s',
handlers=[
logging.FileHandler('.model_lab/monitoring/logs/autosave.log'),
logging.StreamHandler()
]
)
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
# Config
MONITOR_INTERVAL_SECONDS = 15 * 60 # 15 minutos
PROGRESS_JSONL = Path('.model_lab/monitoring/progress.jsonl')
LATEST_CHECKPOINT_JSON = Path('.model_lab/monitoring/latest_checkpoint.json')
class TrainingMonitor:
def __init__(self):
self.progress_jsonl = PROGRESS_JSONL
self.latest_json = LATEST_CHECKPOINT_JSON
self.progress_jsonl.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
self.logs_dir = self.progress_jsonl.parent / 'logs'
self.logs_dir.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
self.step_count = 0
self.start_time = time.time()
def get_checkpoint_metrics(self) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
"""
Lê últimas métricas do arquivo de log do treinamento.
Implementação depende do formato de saída do ModelLab CLI.
"""
try:
# Stub: ler do arquivo de log mais recente
# Exemplo assumindo formato JSON no stdout/stderr
# Para MVP, retornar dados mock enquanto aguarda real logs
checkpoint = {
"checkpoint": {
"run_id": os.getenv("MODELLAB_RUN_ID", "unknown-run"),
"timestamp": datetime.utcnow().isoformat() + "Z",
"step": self.step_count,
"epoch": max(1, self.step_count // 5000), # Assumir 5000 steps/epoch
"total_steps": 5000 * 10, # 10 epochs default
"total_epochs": 10
},
"metrics": {
"loss": 0.45 - (self.step_count / 50000), # Simulated decay
"val_loss": 0.52 - (self.step_count / 45000),
"learning_rate": 0.0002,
"throughput_samples_per_sec": 48.5
},
"resources": {
"gpu_memory_used_gb": self._get_gpu_memory_used(),
"gpu_memory_available_gb": 40.0,
"gpu_utilization_percent": self._get_gpu_utilization(),
"training_time_hours": (time.time() - self.start_time) / 3600
},
"dataset": {
"samples_processed": self.step_count * 2, # Batch size = 2
"samples_total": 240000,
"tokens_per_sample_avg": 128
},
"status": "TRAINING",
"error": None
}
return checkpoint
except Exception as e:
logger.error(f"Erro ao ler métricas: {e}")
return None
def _get_gpu_memory_used(self) -> float:
"""Simula leitura de GPU memory (usar nvidia-smi em produção)"""
try:
# Stub: num produção, rodar: nvidia-smi --query-gpu=memory.used --format=csv,noheader,nounits
return 28.4
except:
return 0.0
def _get_gpu_utilization(self) -> float:
"""Simula utilização de GPU"""
return psutil.cpu_percent(interval=1) * 2 # Proxy simplificado
def save_checkpoint(self, checkpoint: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
"""Salva checkpoint em progress.jsonl e latest_checkpoint.json"""
try:
# Append to JSONL
with open(self.progress_jsonl, 'a') as f:
f.write(json.dumps(checkpoint) + '\n')
# Update latest
with open(self.latest_json, 'w') as f:
json.dump(checkpoint, f, indent=2)
self.step_count += 1
logger.info(f"✓ Checkpoint salvo: step={checkpoint['checkpoint']['step']}")
return True
except Exception as e:
logger.error(f"✗ Erro ao salvar checkpoint: {e}")
return False
def run_loop(self):
"""Loop principal de monitoramento"""
logger.info(f"Iniciando monitor (intervalo: {MONITOR_INTERVAL_SECONDS}s)")
iteration = 0
while True:
try:
iteration += 1
logger.info(f"--- Coleta #{iteration} ---")
checkpoint = self.get_checkpoint_metrics()
if checkpoint:
self.save_checkpoint(checkpoint)
self.export_to_csv()
else:
logger.warning("Não foi possível coletar métricas")
time.sleep(MONITOR_INTERVAL_SECONDS)
except KeyboardInterrupt:
logger.info("Monitor interrompido pelo usuário")
break
except Exception as e:
logger.error(f"Erro no loop principal: {e}")
time.sleep(MONITOR_INTERVAL_SECONDS)
def export_to_csv(self):
"""Exporta progress.jsonl para CSV diário"""
try:
import pandas as pd
# Ler JSONL
with open(self.progress_jsonl) as f:
lines = f.readlines()
data = [json.loads(line) for line in lines]
# Flatten e exportar
df = pd.json_normalize(data)
csv_path = self.progress_jsonl.parent / f"exports/progress_{datetime.now():%Y%m%d}.csv"
csv_path.parent.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
df.to_csv(csv_path, index=False)
logger.info(f"✓ CSV exportado: {csv_path}")
except ImportError:
logger.warning("pandas não instalado, pulando export CSV")
except Exception as e:
logger.error(f"Erro ao exportar CSV: {e}")
def main():
monitor = TrainingMonitor()
monitor.run_loop()
if __name__ == '__main__':
main()
```
### 3.3 Integração: Iniciar Monitor
**Opção 1: Subprocess paralelo (recomendado)**
```bash
# Terminal 1: Iniciar treinamento
modellab convert --model_id microsoft/phi-silica-3.6 --workflow LoRA
# Terminal 2: Iniciar monitor
python .model_lab/scripts/autosave_monitor.py
```
**Opção 2: Script wrapper (automático)**
**Arquivo: `.model_lab/scripts/train_with_monitor.sh`**
```bash
#!/bin/bash
# Inicia treinamento + monitor em paralelo
python .model_lab/scripts/autosave_monitor.py &
MONITOR_PID=$!
modellab convert --model_id microsoft/phi-silica-3.6 --workflow LoRA
TRAIN_EXIT=$?
# Cleanup
kill $MONITOR_PID 2>/dev/null
exit $TRAIN_EXIT
```

# GitHub Copilot Instructions - Ativo Real

## 🏗 Project Architecture
- **Hybrid Monorepo**: React frontend (`src/`) + Python Azure Functions backend (`api/`).
- **Database**: Azure Database for PostgreSQL (Flexible Server) with **PostGIS** extension.
- **Frontend**: React 19 + TypeScript + Vite.
- **Backend**: Azure Functions (Python v2 Model). Defined in `api/function_app.py`.

## 🚀 Development Workflow
- **Frontend**: `npm run dev`
- **Backend (Python)**:
 1. `cd api`
 2. Create/Activate virtual env: `python -m venv .venv`, `.\.venv\Scripts\activate`
 3. Install deps: `pip install -r requirements.txt`
 4. Run: `func start`
- **Env Vars**:
 - `VITE_API_BASE=http://localhost:7071/api` (Frontend)
 - `POSTGRES_CONNECTION_STRING` in `api/local.settings.json` (Backend)

## 🧩 Key Patterns
- **Database Schema**: Managed in `database/schema.sql`. Use PostGIS geometries (`GEOMETRY(Polygon, 4326)`).
- **API (Python)**:
 - Uses `azure-functions` library (v2 model decorators).
 - Uses `psycopg2` for direct DB connection.

### Frontend (React + Maps)
- **Map Components**: Use `React-Leaflet`. Main view: `src/components/Map/MapView.tsx`.
- **Styling**: Mixed approach.
 - Utility classes: **Tailwind CSS**.
 - Complex components: **CSS Modules** (e.g., `MapView.css`, `LayerControl.css`).
- **State Management**: Local React state + Props.
- **API Service**: Centralized in `src/services/onboardingService.ts`. Use generic `postJson` wrapper.

### Data Types
- **GeoJSON**: Used for map layers. Parsed via `togeojson`.
- **Layer Interfaces**: Defined in `MapView.tsx` (`MarkerData`, `PolygonData`, `MapLayer`).

## ⚠️ Critical Files
- `functions.js`: Backend entry point. DO NOT DELETE.
- `src/functions/index.js`: Registers function handlers.
- `src/components/Map/MapView.tsx`: Core map logic and layer rendering.
- `local.settings.json`: Local backend settings (excluded from git).

## 🧪 Testing
- **Unit**: `npm test` (Vitest).
- **Manual**: Use `src/mocks/mockServer.js` or run full backend with `npm start` to test API flows.
# GitHub Copilot Instructions - Ativo Real
## 🏗 Architecture Overview
**Ativo Real** is a geo-asset management platform for rural and urban properties in Brazil. Three distinct user profiles drive the UX:
- **Topógrafo** (Surveyor): Technical drawing and property boundary measurement
- **Proprietário** (Owner): View and manage property assets
- **Agricultor** (Farmer): Agricultural operations and land management
**Tech Stack**: React 19 + TypeScript + Vite (frontend) · Python Azure Functions v2 (backend) · PostgreSQL + PostGIS (spatial database)
## 🚀 Development Workflow
### Frontend (React)
```bash
npm install
npm run dev
```
- Runs on `http://localhost:5173` (Vite default)
- Hot reload enabled for `.tsx` and `.ts` files
- TypeScript strict mode (no `any` allowed in new code)
### Backend (Python)
```bash
cd api
python -m venv .venv
# Windows: .\.venv\Scripts\activate
# macOS/Linux: source .venv/bin/activate
pip install -r requirements.txt
func start
```
- Azure Functions runtime on `http://localhost:7071`
- API endpoints exposed at `/api/*` routes
### Environment Variables
```
VITE_API_BASE=http://localhost:7071/api # Frontend → Backend
POSTGRES_CONNECTION_STRING=... # Backend → DB (in local.settings.json)
VITE_AZURE_MAPS_KEY=... # For interactive maps
```
## 🧩 Key Architectural Patterns
### Data Flow: Frontend → Backend → DB
1. **User Action** (e.g., submit form in `RuralForm.tsx`)
2. **API Call** via `onboardingService.ts` → `postJson()` helper wraps all requests
3. **Python Handler** in `api/function_app.py` validates & processes
4. **DB Operation** using `psycopg2` to PostgreSQL, returns JSON response
5. **Frontend State** updates via React hooks (`useState`)
### Geographic Data Model (PostGIS)
All properties stored with `GEOMETRY(Polygon, 4326)` in `propriedades` table ([database/schema.sql](database/schema.sql)):
- SRID 4326 = WGS84 lat/lng coordinates
- Used for spatial queries, boundary visualization, and area calculations
- Related metadata stored in `JSONB` columns (`metadados_rurais`, `metadados_urbanos`)
### Frontend Component Hierarchy
```
App.tsx (landing/app view selector)
├── GlobalMap.tsx (map + layer control)
│ ├── RuralForm.tsx (farm registration)
│ ├── UrbanForm.tsx (property activation)
│ └── TechLoginForm.tsx (surveyor login)
└── Footer.tsx
```
### Profile-Driven Routing
- [App.tsx](src/App.tsx) manages `currentView` ('landing' → 'app') and `userProfile` state
- Forms rendered conditionally based on selected profile
- Each profile maps to a separate onboarding endpoint:
- `POST /api/rural/onboard` → `RuralForm.tsx`
- `POST /api/urban/activate` → `UrbanForm.tsx`
- `POST /api/tech/login` → `TechLoginForm.tsx`
## 💾 Component and Service Locations
| Purpose | Files |
|---------|-------|
| **Onboarding API** | [src/services/onboardingService.ts](src/services/onboardingService.ts) (single source for all POST calls) |
| **Form Submissions** | `src/components/Forms/{RuralForm, UrbanForm, TechLoginForm}.tsx` |
| **Map & Layers** | [src/GlobalMap.tsx](src/GlobalMap.tsx) (main logic) + leaflet integration |
| **UI Primitives** | `src/components/ui/{Button, FormField, Toast, Footer}.tsx` |
| **DB Schema** | [database/schema.sql](database/schema.sql) (PostGIS-enabled, handles rural & urban metadata) |
| **API Handlers** | [api/function_app.py](api/function_app.py) (Python v2 decorators for routes) |
| **DB Setup** | [api/db_setup.py](api/db_setup.py) (schema initialization on first function call) |
## 🎨 Styling Approach
- **Tailwind CSS**: For utility-first layout (`className="flex gap-4 ..."`). Primary layout & spacing.
- **CSS Modules**: For complex component styling (reserved for future `MapView.module.css` if needed).
- **Inline Styles** (`style={...}`): Used in [App.tsx](src/App.tsx) for hero section & profile cards (legacy, not recommended for new code).
- **New Code**: Default to Tailwind utilities; use CSS Modules only if Tailwind can't express the design.
## ⚠️ Critical Constraints & Patterns
1. **No `react-leaflet` yet**: Current [GlobalMap.tsx](src/GlobalMap.tsx) uses raw Leaflet. If adding interactive map controls, follow existing patterns.
2. **No External UI Framework**: All buttons/forms are custom (`src/components/ui/`). Extend these instead of adding external libraries.
3. **Type Safety**: All API responses typed in [onboardingService.ts](src/services/onboardingService.ts) (e.g., `RegisterRuralFarmResult`, `OnboardingSnapshot`).
4. **Error Handling**: Forms handle HTTP errors gracefully via `postJson()` error block; display errors via `Toast` component.
5. **Database Connection**: Pooling handled by Azure Functions runtime. [db_setup.py](api/db_setup.py) ensures schema exists on first call.
6. **PostGIS Geometry**: Always SRID 4326 for GPS coordinates. Use `ST_AsGeoJSON()` for frontend conversion.
## 🚫 Do Not
- Delete `functions.js` or `src/functions/index.js` (legacy Azure Functions entry points, still needed)
- Add external UI libraries (Shadcn, MUI, etc.) without discussion
- Use `any` type in TypeScript
- Store sensitive config in code (use `local.settings.json` or Azure Key Vault)
- Modify [database/schema.sql](database/schema.sql) without understanding PostGIS implications
## 🧪 Testing & Debug
- **Frontend**: `npm run lint` (eslint, no tests yet), manual testing via `npm run dev`
- **Backend**: Mock DB fallback in `get_db_connection()` for local testing without PostgreSQL
- **Full Stack**: Run both `npm run dev` (frontend) and `func start` (backend) simultaneously; test flows end-to-end
- **GeoJSON**: Use `togeojson` library to parse KML/CSV imports into GeoJSON for rendering
## 📝 Code Conventions
- **Imports**: Absolute paths not yet configured; use relative imports (`../services/...`)
- **Functions**: Use arrow functions in React; decorator-based for Python Azure Functions
- **Comments**: Explain "why", not "what" (code should be readable)
- **Commit Messages**: Include the user profile affected (e.g., "feat: add Topógrafo measurement tool")

# GitHub Copilot Instructions - Ativo Real

## 🏗 Architecture Overview
**Ativo Real** is a geo-asset management platform for rural and urban properties in Brazil. Three distinct user profiles drive the UX:
- **Topógrafo** (Surveyor): Technical drawing and property boundary measurement
- **Proprietário** (Owner): View and manage property assets
- **Agricultor** (Farmer): Agricultural operations and land management

**Tech Stack**: React 19 + TypeScript + Vite (frontend) · Python Azure Functions v2 (backend) · PostgreSQL + PostGIS (spatial database)

## 🚀 Development Workflow

### Frontend (React)
```bash
npm install
npm run dev
```
- Runs on `http://localhost:5173` (Vite default)
- Hot reload enabled for `.tsx` and `.ts` files
- TypeScript strict mode (no `any` allowed in new code)

### Backend (Python)
```bash
cd api
python -m venv .venv
# Windows: .\.venv\Scripts\activate
# macOS/Linux: source .venv/bin/activate
pip install -r requirements.txt
func start
```
- Azure Functions runtime on `http://localhost:7071`
- API endpoints exposed at `/api/*` routes

### Environment Variables
```
VITE_API_BASE=http://localhost:7071/api # Frontend → Backend
POSTGRES_CONNECTION_STRING=... # Backend → DB (in local.settings.json)
VITE_AZURE_MAPS_KEY=... # For interactive maps
```

## 🧩 Key Architectural Patterns

### Data Flow: Frontend → Backend → DB
1. **User Action** (e.g., submit form in `RuralForm.tsx`)
2. **API Call** via `onboardingService.ts` → `postJson()` helper wraps all requests
3. **Python Handler** in `api/function_app.py` validates & processes
4. **DB Operation** using `psycopg2` to PostgreSQL, returns JSON response
5. **Frontend State** updates via React hooks (`useState`)

### Geographic Data Model (PostGIS)
All properties stored with `GEOMETRY(Polygon, 4326)` in `propriedades` table ([database/schema.sql](database/schema.sql)):
- SRID 4326 = WGS84 lat/lng coordinates
- Used for spatial queries, boundary visualization, and area calculations
- Related metadata stored in `JSONB` columns (`metadados_rurais`, `metadados_urbanos`)

### Frontend Component Hierarchy
```
App.tsx (landing/app view selector)
├── GlobalMap.tsx (map + layer control)
│ ├── RuralForm.tsx (farm registration)
│ ├── UrbanForm.tsx (property activation)
│ └── TechLoginForm.tsx (surveyor login)
└── Footer.tsx
```

### Profile-Driven Routing
- [App.tsx](src/App.tsx) manages `currentView` ('landing' → 'app') and `userProfile` state
- Forms rendered conditionally based on selected profile
- Each profile maps to a separate onboarding endpoint:
 - `POST /api/rural/onboard` → `RuralForm.tsx`
 - `POST /api/urban/activate` → `UrbanForm.tsx`
 - `POST /api/tech/login` → `TechLoginForm.tsx`

## 💾 Component and Service Locations

| Purpose | Files |
|---------|-------|
| **Onboarding API** | [src/services/onboardingService.ts](src/services/onboardingService.ts) (single source for all POST calls) |
| **Form Submissions** | `src/components/Forms/{RuralForm, UrbanForm, TechLoginForm}.tsx` |
| **Map & Layers** | [src/GlobalMap.tsx](src/GlobalMap.tsx) (main logic) + leaflet integration |
| **UI Primitives** | `src/components/ui/{Button, FormField, Toast, Footer}.tsx` |
| **DB Schema** | [database/schema.sql](database/schema.sql) (PostGIS-enabled, handles rural & urban metadata) |
| **API Handlers** | [api/function_app.py](api/function_app.py) (Python v2 decorators for routes) |
| **DB Setup** | [api/db_setup.py](api/db_setup.py) (schema initialization on first function call) |

## 🎨 Styling Approach
- **Tailwind CSS**: For utility-first layout (`className="flex gap-4 ..."`). Primary layout & spacing.
- **CSS Modules**: For complex component styling (reserved for future `MapView.module.css` if needed).
- **Inline Styles** (`style={...}`): Used in [App.tsx](src/App.tsx) for hero section & profile cards (legacy, not recommended for new code).
- **New Code**: Default to Tailwind utilities; use CSS Modules only if Tailwind can't express the design.

## ⚠️ Critical Constraints & Patterns
1. **No `react-leaflet` yet**: Current [GlobalMap.tsx](src/GlobalMap.tsx) uses raw Leaflet. If adding interactive map controls, follow existing patterns.
2. **No External UI Framework**: All buttons/forms are custom (`src/components/ui/`). Extend these instead of adding external libraries.
3. **Type Safety**: All API responses typed in [onboardingService.ts](src/services/onboardingService.ts) (e.g., `RegisterRuralFarmResult`, `OnboardingSnapshot`).
4. **Error Handling**: Forms handle HTTP errors gracefully via `postJson()` error block; display errors via `Toast` component.
5. **Database Connection**: Pooling handled by Azure Functions runtime. [db_setup.py](api/db_setup.py) ensures schema exists on first call.
6. **PostGIS Geometry**: Always SRID 4326 for GPS coordinates. Use `ST_AsGeoJSON()` for frontend conversion.

## 🚫 Do Not
- Delete `functions.js` or `src/functions/index.js` (legacy Azure Functions entry points, still needed)
- Add external UI libraries (Shadcn, MUI, etc.) without discussion
- Use `any` type in TypeScript
- Store sensitive config in code (use `local.settings.json` or Azure Key Vault)
- Modify [database/schema.sql](database/schema.sql) without understanding PostGIS implications

## 🧪 Testing & Debug
- **Frontend**: `npm run lint` (eslint, no tests yet), manual testing via `npm run dev`
- **Backend**: Mock DB fallback in `get_db_connection()` for local testing without PostgreSQL
- **Full Stack**: Run both `npm run dev` (frontend) and `func start` (backend) simultaneously; test flows end-to-end
- **GeoJSON**: Use `togeojson` library to parse KML/CSV imports into GeoJSON for rendering

## 📝 Code Conventions
- **Imports**: Absolute paths not yet configured; use relative imports (`../services/...`)
- **Functions**: Use arrow functions in React; decorator-based for Python Azure Functions
- **Comments**: Explain "why", not "what" (code should be readable)
- **Commit Messages**: Include the user profile affected (e.g., "feat: add Topógrafo measurement tool")

# GitHub Copilot Instructions - Ativo Real

## 🏗 Project Architecture
- **Hybrid Monorepo**: React frontend (`src/`) + Python Azure Functions backend (`api/`).
- **Database**: Azure Database for PostgreSQL (Flexible Server) with **PostGIS** extension.
- **Frontend**: React 19 + TypeScript + Vite.
- **Backend**: Azure Functions (Python v2 Model). Defined in `api/function_app.py`.

## 🚀 Development Workflow
- **Frontend**: `npm run dev`
- **Backend (Python)**:
 1. `cd api`
 2. Create/Activate virtual env: `python -m venv .venv`, `.\.venv\Scripts\activate`
 3. Install deps: `pip install -r requirements.txt`
 4. Run: `func start`
- **Env Vars**:
 - `VITE_API_BASE=http://localhost:7071/api` (Frontend)
 - `POSTGRES_CONNECTION_STRING` in `api/local.settings.json` (Backend)

## 🧩 Key Patterns
- **Database Schema**: Managed in `database/schema.sql`. Use PostGIS geometries (`GEOMETRY(Polygon, 4326)`).
- **API (Python)**:
 - Uses `azure-functions` library (v2 model decorators).
 - Uses `psycopg2` for direct DB connection.

### Frontend (React + Maps)
- **Map Components**: Use `React-Leaflet`. Main view: `src/components/Map/MapView.tsx`.
- **Styling**: Mixed approach.
 - Utility classes: **Tailwind CSS**.
 - Complex components: **CSS Modules** (e.g., `MapView.css`, `LayerControl.css`).
- **State Management**: Local React state + Props.
- **API Service**: Centralized in `src/services/onboardingService.ts`. Use generic `postJson` wrapper.

### Data Types
- **GeoJSON**: Used for map layers. Parsed via `togeojson`.
- **Layer Interfaces**: Defined in `MapView.tsx` (`MarkerData`, `PolygonData`, `MapLayer`).

## ⚠️ Critical Files
- `functions.js`: Backend entry point. DO NOT DELETE.
- `src/functions/index.js`: Registers function handlers.
- `src/components/Map/MapView.tsx`: Core map logic and layer rendering.
- `local.settings.json`: Local backend settings (excluded from git).

## 🧪 Testing
- **Unit**: `npm test` (Vitest).
- **Manual**: Use `src/mocks/mockServer.js` or run full backend with `npm start` to test API flows.

### Profile-Driven Routing & Tool Activation
- [App.tsx](src/App.tsx) manages `currentView` ('landing' → 'app') and `userProfile` state
- Each profile triggers different UX:
 - **Topógrafo**: Advanced map tools (draw, measure, layer management, vertex editing)
 - **Proprietário**: Passive map visualization with property overlay
 - **Agricultor**: Map with CAR/productivity metadata overlays
- Each profile maps to separate API endpoints:
 - `POST /api/rural/onboard` → `RuralForm.tsx`
 - `POST /api/urban/activate` → `UrbanForm.tsx`
 - `POST /api/tech/login` → `TechLoginForm.tsx`

### Map Architecture (OpenLayers 10.7.0)
[GlobalMap.tsx](src/GlobalMap.tsx) manages:
- **Base layers**: Satellite (Esri), Street (OSM), configurable via XYZ tile sources
- **Vector layers**: Polygons, markers, circles, GeoJSON imports (KML/GeoJSON/CSV/TXT via `togeojson`, `shpjs`)
- **Interactions**: Draw (polygon/line/circle), Modify (vertex editing), Snap (automatic alignment), Select (feature inspection)
- **Popup management**: Click-to-inspect features via `Overlay` component with custom popup HTML
- **Styling system**: Classified styles (cliente/vizinho/padrão) with color coding, fill transparency, and text labels
- **Projection handling**: proj4 integration for UTM conversions (EPSG:31983 for Brazil zone 23S / SIRGAS 2000)

## 🔄 Evolution & Variations

The [bem-real](../../../Desktop/Backup%20SSD/bem-real/ativo-real/) folder contains an evolved version with:
- **DashboardTopografo.tsx**: Project/artifact management for surveyors
- **AIBotChat.tsx**: Integrated chatbot with context-aware prompts
- **InfinitePayModal.tsx**: Payment gateway integration (PIX, card, boleto)
- **Design Tokens**: CSS custom property system for theming (colors, spacing)
- **Enhanced SVG Icons**: 39+ topographic icons (Bronze #CD7F32, 2px stroke)
- **GlobalMapValidacao.tsx**: Validation & analysis features for surveyor reports
- **Expanded Services**: ai_assistant.py, infinitepay_payment.py, function_app_cosmosdb.py

Refer to bem-real for advanced patterns, but maintain backward compatibility with the simpler ativo-real structure.

# GitHub Copilot Instructions - Ativo Real
## 🏗 Architecture Overview
**Ativo Real** is a geo-asset management platform for rural and urban properties in Brazil. Three distinct user profiles drive the UX:
- **Topógrafo** (Surveyor): Technical drawing and property boundary measurement
- **Proprietário** (Owner): View and manage property assets
- **Agricultor** (Farmer): Agricultural operations and land management
**Tech Stack**: React 19 + TypeScript + Vite (frontend) · Python Azure Functions v2 (backend) · PostgreSQL + PostGIS (spatial database)
## 🚀 Development Workflow
### Frontend (React)
```bash
npm install
npm run dev
```
- Runs on `http://localhost:5173` (Vite default)
- Hot reload enabled for `.tsx` and `.ts` files
- TypeScript strict mode (no `any` allowed in new code)
### Backend (Python)
```bash
cd api
python -m venv .venv
# Windows: .\.venv\Scripts\activate
# macOS/Linux: source .venv/bin/activate
pip install -r requirements.txt
func start
```
- Azure Functions runtime on `http://localhost:7071`
- API endpoints exposed at `/api/*` routes
### Environment Variables
```
VITE_API_BASE=http://localhost:7071/api # Frontend → Backend
POSTGRES_CONNECTION_STRING=... # Backend → DB (in local.settings.json)
VITE_AZURE_MAPS_KEY=... # For interactive maps
```
## 🧩 Key Architectural Patterns
### Data Flow: Frontend → Backend → DB
1. **User Action** (e.g., submit form in `RuralForm.tsx`)
2. **API Call** via `onboardingService.ts` → `postJson()` helper wraps all requests
3. **Python Handler** in `api/function_app.py` validates & processes
4. **DB Operation** using `psycopg2` to PostgreSQL, returns JSON response
5. **Frontend State** updates via React hooks (`useState`)
### Geographic Data Model (PostGIS)
All properties stored with `GEOMETRY(Polygon, 4326)` in `propriedades` table ([database/schema.sql](database/schema.sql)):
- SRID 4326 = WGS84 lat/lng coordinates
- Used for spatial queries, boundary visualization, and area calculations
- Related metadata stored in `JSONB` columns (`metadados_rurais`, `metadados_urbanos`)
### Frontend Component Hierarchy
```
App.tsx (landing/app view selector)
├── GlobalMap.tsx (map + layer control)
│ ├── RuralForm.tsx (farm registration)
│ ├── UrbanForm.tsx (property activation)
│ └── TechLoginForm.tsx (surveyor login)
└── Footer.tsx
```
### Profile-Driven Routing
- [App.tsx](src/App.tsx) manages `currentView` ('landing' → 'app') and `userProfile` state
- Forms rendered conditionally based on selected profile
- Each profile maps to a separate onboarding endpoint:
- `POST /api/rural/onboard` → `RuralForm.tsx`
- `POST /api/urban/activate` → `UrbanForm.tsx`
- `POST /api/tech/login` → `TechLoginForm.tsx`
## 💾 Component and Service Locations
| Purpose | Files |
|---------|-------|
| **Onboarding API** | [src/services/onboardingService.ts](src/services/onboardingService.ts) (single source for all POST calls) |
| **Form Submissions** | `src/components/Forms/{RuralForm, UrbanForm, TechLoginForm}.tsx` |
| **Map & Layers** | [src/GlobalMap.tsx](src/GlobalMap.tsx) (main logic) + leaflet integration |
| **UI Primitives** | `src/components/ui/{Button, FormField, Toast, Footer}.tsx` |
| **DB Schema** | [database/schema.sql](database/schema.sql) (PostGIS-enabled, handles rural & urban metadata) |
| **API Handlers** | [api/function_app.py](api/function_app.py) (Python v2 decorators for routes) |
| **DB Setup** | [api/db_setup.py](api/db_setup.py) (schema initialization on first function call) |
## 🎨 Styling Approach
- **Tailwind CSS**: For utility-first layout (`className="flex gap-4 ..."`). Primary layout & spacing.
- **CSS Modules**: For complex component styling (reserved for future `MapView.module.css` if needed).
- **Inline Styles** (`style={...}`): Used in [App.tsx](src/App.tsx) for hero section & profile cards (legacy, not recommended for new code).
- **New Code**: Default to Tailwind utilities; use CSS Modules only if Tailwind can't express the design.
## ⚠️ Critical Constraints & Patterns
1. **No `react-leaflet` yet**: Current [GlobalMap.tsx](src/GlobalMap.tsx) uses raw Leaflet. If adding interactive map controls, follow existing patterns.
2. **No External UI Framework**: All buttons/forms are custom (`src/components/ui/`). Extend these instead of adding external libraries.
3. **Type Safety**: All API responses typed in [onboardingService.ts](src/services/onboardingService.ts) (e.g., `RegisterRuralFarmResult`, `OnboardingSnapshot`).
4. **Error Handling**: Forms handle HTTP errors gracefully via `postJson()` error block; display errors via `Toast` component.
5. **Database Connection**: Pooling handled by Azure Functions runtime. [db_setup.py](api/db_setup.py) ensures schema exists on first call.
6. **PostGIS Geometry**: Always SRID 4326 for GPS coordinates. Use `ST_AsGeoJSON()` for frontend conversion.
## 🚫 Do Not
- Delete `functions.js` or `src/functions/index.js` (legacy Azure Functions entry points, still needed)
- Add external UI libraries (Shadcn, MUI, etc.) without discussion
- Use `any` type in TypeScript
- Store sensitive config in code (use `local.settings.json` or Azure Key Vault)
- Modify [database/schema.sql](database/schema.sql) without understanding PostGIS implications
## 🧪 Testing & Debug
- **Frontend**: `npm run lint` (eslint, no tests yet), manual testing via `npm run dev`
- **Backend**: Mock DB fallback in `get_db_connection()` for local testing without PostgreSQL
- **Full Stack**: Run both `npm run dev` (frontend) and `func start` (backend) simultaneously; test flows end-to-end
- **GeoJSON**: Use `togeojson` library to parse KML/CSV imports into GeoJSON for rendering
## 📝 Code Conventions
- **Imports**: Absolute paths not yet configured; use relative imports (`../services/...`)
- **Functions**: Use arrow functions in React; decorator-based for Python Azure Functions
- **Comments**: Explain "why", not "what" (code should be readable)
- **Commit Messages**: Include the user profile affected (e.g., "feat: add Topógrafo measurement tool")

### Profile-Driven Routing & Tool Activation
- [App.tsx](src/App.tsx) manages `currentView` ('landing' → 'app') and `userProfile` state
- Each profile triggers different UX:
 - **Topógrafo**: Advanced map tools (draw, measure, layer management, vertex editing)
 - **Proprietário**: Passive map visualization with property overlay
 - **Agricultor**: Map with CAR/productivity metadata overlays
- Each profile maps to separate API endpoints:
 - `POST /api/rural/onboard` → `RuralForm.tsx`
 - `POST /api/urban/activate` → `UrbanForm.tsx`
 - `POST /api/tech/login` → `TechLoginForm.tsx`

### Map Architecture (OpenLayers 10.7.0)
[GlobalMap.tsx](src/GlobalMap.tsx) manages:
- **Base layers**: Satellite (Esri), Street (OSM), configurable via XYZ tile sources
- **Vector layers**: Polygons, markers, circles, GeoJSON imports (KML/GeoJSON/CSV/TXT via `togeojson`, `shpjs`)
- **Interactions**: Draw (polygon/line/circle), Modify (vertex editing), Snap (automatic alignment), Select (feature inspection)
- **Popup management**: Click-to-inspect features via `Overlay` component with custom popup HTML
- **Styling system**: Classified styles (cliente/vizinho/padrão) with color coding, fill transparency, and text labels
- **Projection handling**: proj4 integration for UTM conversions (EPSG:31983 for Brazil zone 23S / SIRGAS 2000)
OpenLayers
use if needed).
- **Inline Styles** (`style={...}`): Used in [App.tsx](src/App.tsx) for hero section & profile cards (legacy, not recommended for new code).
- **SVG Imports**: Use ES6 `import` syntax (configured via Vite SVG plugin); avoid `require()`.
- **New Code**: Default to Tailwind utilities; use CSS Modules only if Tailwind canno
OpenLayers API. If adding interactive map controls, follow existing class/hook patterns.
2. **No External UI Framework**: All buttons/forms are custom (`src/components/ui/`). Extend these instead of adding external libraries like Shadcn, MUI.
3. **Type Safety**: All API responses typed in [onboardingService.ts](src/services/onboardingService.ts) (e.g., `RegisterRuralFarmParams`, `OnboardingSnapshot`).
4. **Error Handling**: Forms handle HTTP errors gracefully via `postJson()` error block; display errors via `Toast` component.
5. **Database Connection**: Pooling handled by Azure Functions runtime. [db_setup.py](api/db_setup.py) ensures schema exists on first call.
6. **PostGIS Geometry**: Always SRID 4326 for GPS coordinates. Use `ST_AsGeoJSON()` for frontend conversion.
7. **Projection Handling** (SIRGAS 2000): proj4 setup in [GlobalMap.tsx](src/GlobalMap.tsx) for UTM zone 23S. Use for surveyor measurements; store GPS coords in DB
- Use `require()` for SVG/asset imports—use ES6 `import` (configured via Vite SVG plugin)
- **SQL Debugging**: Connection string format: `"dbname=... user=... password=... host=... sslmode=require"` (Azure PostgreSQL)
## 🔄 Evolution & Variations

The [bem-real](../../../Desktop/Backup%20SSD/bem-real/ativo-real/) folder contains an evolved version with:
- **DashboardTopografo.tsx**: Project/artifact management for surveyors
- **AIBotChat.tsx**: Integrated chatbot with context-aware prompts
- **InfinitePayModal.tsx**: Payment gateway integration (PIX, card, boleto)
- **Design Tokens**: CSS custom property system for theming (colors, spacing)
- **Enhanced SVG Icons**: 39+ topographic icons (Bronze #CD7F32, 2px stroke)
- **GlobalMapValidacao.tsx**: Validation & analysis features for surveyor reports
- **Expanded Services**: ai_assistant.py, infinitepay_payment.py, function_app_cosmosdb.py

Refer to bem-real for advanced patterns, but maintain backward compatibility with the simpler ativo-real structure.

# GitHub Copilot Instructions - Ativo Real

## 🏗 Project Architecture
- **Hybrid Monorepo**: React frontend (`src/`) + Python Azure Functions backend (`api/`).
- **Database**: Azure Database for PostgreSQL (Flexible Server) with **PostGIS** extension.
- **Frontend**: React 19 + TypeScript + Vite.
- **Backend**: Azure Functions (Python v2 Model). Defined in `api/function_app.py`.

## 🚀 Development Workflow
- **Frontend**: `npm run dev`
- **Backend (Python)**:
 1. `cd api`
 2. Create/Activate virtual env: `python -m venv .venv`, `.\.venv\Scripts\activate`
 3. Install deps: `pip install -r requirements.txt`
 4. Run: `func start`
- **Env Vars**:
 - `VITE_API_BASE=http://localhost:7071/api` (Frontend)
 - `POSTGRES_CONNECTION_STRING` in `api/local.settings.json` (Backend)

## 🧩 Key Patterns
- **Database Schema**: Managed in `database/schema.sql`. Use PostGIS geometries (`GEOMETRY(Polygon, 4326)`).
- **API (Python)**:
 - Uses `azure-functions` library (v2 model decorators).
 - Uses `psycopg2` for direct DB connection.

### Frontend (React + Maps)
- **Map Components**: Use `React-Leaflet`. Main view: `src/components/Map/MapView.tsx`.
- **Styling**: Mixed approach.
 - Utility classes: **Tailwind CSS**.
 - Complex components: **CSS Modules** (e.g., `MapView.css`, `LayerControl.css`).
- **State Management**: Local React state + Props.
- **API Service**: Centralized in `src/services/onboardingService.ts`. Use generic `postJson` wrapper.

### Data Types
- **GeoJSON**: Used for map layers. Parsed via `togeojson`.
- **Layer Interfaces**: Defined in `MapView.tsx` (`MarkerData`, `PolygonData`, `MapLayer`).

## ⚠️ Critical Files
- `functions.js`: Backend entry point. DO NOT DELETE.
- `src/functions/index.js`: Registers function handlers.
- `src/components/Map/MapView.tsx`: Core map logic and layer rendering.
- `local.settings.json`: Local backend settings (excluded from git).

## 🧪 Testing
- **Unit**: `npm test` (Vitest).
- **Manual**: Use `src/mocks/mockServer.js` or run full backend with `npm start` to test API flows.
# GitHub Copilot Instructions - Ativo Real
## 🏗 Architecture Overview
**Ativo Real** is a geo-asset management platform for rural and urban properties in Brazil. Three distinct user profiles drive the UX:
- **Topógrafo** (Surveyor): Technical drawing and property boundary measurement
- **Proprietário** (Owner): View and manage property assets
- **Agricultor** (Farmer): Agricultural operations and land management
**Tech Stack**: React 19 + TypeScript + Vite (frontend) · Python Azure Functions v2 (backend) · PostgreSQL + PostGIS (spatial database)
## 🚀 Development Workflow
### Frontend (React)
```bash
npm install
npm run dev
```
- Runs on `http://localhost:5173` (Vite default)
- Hot reload enabled for `.tsx` and `.ts` files
- TypeScript strict mode (no `any` allowed in new code)
### Backend (Python)
```bash
cd api
python -m venv .venv
# Windows: .\.venv\Scripts\activate
# macOS/Linux: source .venv/bin/activate
pip install -r requirements.txt
func start
```
- Azure Functions runtime on `http://localhost:7071`
- API endpoints exposed at `/api/*` routes
### Environment Variables
```
VITE_API_BASE=http://localhost:7071/api # Frontend → Backend
POSTGRES_CONNECTION_STRING=... # Backend → DB (in local.settings.json)
VITE_AZURE_MAPS_KEY=... # For interactive maps
```
## 🧩 Key Architectural Patterns
### Data Flow: Frontend → Backend → DB
1. **User Action** (e.g., submit form in `RuralForm.tsx`)
2. **API Call** via `onboardingService.ts` → `postJson()` helper wraps all requests
3. **Python Handler** in `api/function_app.py` validates & processes
4. **DB Operation** using `psycopg2` to PostgreSQL, returns JSON response
5. **Frontend State** updates via React hooks (`useState`)
### Geographic Data Model (PostGIS)
All properties stored with `GEOMETRY(Polygon, 4326)` in `propriedades` table ([database/schema.sql](database/schema.sql)):
- SRID 4326 = WGS84 lat/lng coordinates
- Used for spatial queries, boundary visualization, and area calculations
- Related metadata stored in `JSONB` columns (`metadados_rurais`, `metadados_urbanos`)
### Frontend Component Hierarchy
```
App.tsx (landing/app view selector)
├── GlobalMap.tsx (map + layer control)
│ ├── RuralForm.tsx (farm registration)
│ ├── UrbanForm.tsx (property activation)
│ └── TechLoginForm.tsx (surveyor login)
└── Footer.tsx
```
### Profile-Driven Routing
- [App.tsx](src/App.tsx) manages `currentView` ('landing' → 'app') and `userProfile` state
- Forms rendered conditionally based on selected profile
- Each profile maps to a separate onboarding endpoint:
- `POST /api/rural/onboard` → `RuralForm.tsx`
- `POST /api/urban/activate` → `UrbanForm.tsx`
- `POST /api/tech/login` → `TechLoginForm.tsx`
## 💾 Component and Service Locations
| Purpose | Files |
|---------|-------|
| **Onboarding API** | [src/services/onboardingService.ts](src/services/onboardingService.ts) (single source for all POST calls) |
| **Form Submissions** | `src/components/Forms/{RuralForm, UrbanForm, TechLoginForm}.tsx` |
| **Map & Layers** | [src/GlobalMap.tsx](src/GlobalMap.tsx) (main logic) + leaflet integration |
| **UI Primitives** | `src/components/ui/{Button, FormField, Toast, Footer}.tsx` |
| **DB Schema** | [database/schema.sql](database/schema.sql) (PostGIS-enabled, handles rural & urban metadata) |
| **API Handlers** | [api/function_app.py](api/function_app.py) (Python v2 decorators for routes) |
| **DB Setup** | [api/db_setup.py](api/db_setup.py) (schema initialization on first function call) |
## 🎨 Styling Approach
- **Tailwind CSS**: For utility-first layout (`className="flex gap-4 ..."`). Primary layout & spacing.
- **CSS Modules**: For complex component styling (reserved for future `MapView.module.css` if needed).
- **Inline Styles** (`style={...}`): Used in [App.tsx](src/App.tsx) for hero section & profile cards (legacy, not recommended for new code).
- **New Code**: Default to Tailwind utilities; use CSS Modules only if Tailwind can't express the design.
## ⚠️ Critical Constraints & Patterns
1. **No `react-leaflet` yet**: Current [GlobalMap.tsx](src/GlobalMap.tsx) uses raw Leaflet. If adding interactive map controls, follow existing patterns.
2. **No External UI Framework**: All buttons/forms are custom (`src/components/ui/`). Extend these instead of adding external libraries.
3. **Type Safety**: All API responses typed in [onboardingService.ts](src/services/onboardingService.ts) (e.g., `RegisterRuralFarmResult`, `OnboardingSnapshot`).
4. **Error Handling**: Forms handle HTTP errors gracefully via `postJson()` error block; display errors via `Toast` component.
5. **Database Connection**: Pooling handled by Azure Functions runtime. [db_setup.py](api/db_setup.py) ensures schema exists on first call.
6. **PostGIS Geometry**: Always SRID 4326 for GPS coordinates. Use `ST_AsGeoJSON()` for frontend conversion.
## 🚫 Do Not
- Delete `functions.js` or `src/functions/index.js` (legacy Azure Functions entry points, still needed)
- Add external UI libraries (Shadcn, MUI, etc.) without discussion
- Use `any` type in TypeScript
- Store sensitive config in code (use `local.settings.json` or Azure Key Vault)
- Modify [database/schema.sql](database/schema.sql) without understanding PostGIS implications
## 🧪 Testing & Debug
- **Frontend**: `npm run lint` (eslint, no tests yet), manual testing via `npm run dev`
- **Backend**: Mock DB fallback in `get_db_connection()` for local testing without PostgreSQL
- **Full Stack**: Run both `npm run dev` (frontend) and `func start` (backend) simultaneously; test flows end-to-end
- **GeoJSON**: Use `togeojson` library to parse KML/CSV imports into GeoJSON for rendering
## 📝 Code Conventions
- **Imports**: Absolute paths not yet configured; use relative imports (`../services/...`)
- **Functions**: Use arrow functions in React; decorator-based for Python Azure Functions
- **Comments**: Explain "why", not "what" (code should be readable)
- **Commit Messages**: Include the user profile affected (e.g., "feat: add Topógrafo measurement tool")

Para configurar o Git, você pode executar os seguintes comandos no terminal (PowerShell):

**1. Configurar identidade global (nome e email):**
```powershell
git config --global user.name "Seu Nome"
git config --global user.email "seu.email@example.com"
```

**2. Se ainda não tiver inicializado o repositório:**
```powershell
cd C:\Users\User\progresso\ativo-real
git init
```

**3. Adicionar um remote (se tiver um repositório no GitHub/GitLab):**
```powershell
git remote add origin https://github.com/seu-usuario/seu-repositorio.git
```

**4. Fazer o primeiro commit:**
```powershell
git add .
git commit -m "Initial commit"
```

**5. Enviar para o repositório remoto:**
```powershell
git branch -M main
git push -u origin main
```

**Qual é seu cenário?**
- Quer inicializar um novo repositório?
- Já tem um repositório remoto (GitHub, GitLab, etc.)?
- Quer apenas configurar a identidade do Git?

Entendi! Você quer silenciar a janela do VS Code. Aqui estão as opções:

**Para silenciar notificações:**

Use o comando **Notifications: Hide Notifications** para ocultar as notificações.

```json
[
 {
 "type": "command",
 "details": {
 "key": "notifications.hideList"
 }
 }
]
```

**Para desabilitar notificações do sistema:**

Use a configuração **chat.notifyWindowOnConfirmation** para desabilitar notificações do sistema quando a janela não está em foco.

```json
[
 {
 "type": "setting",
 "details": {
 "key": "chat.notifyWindowOnConfirmation",
 "value": "false"
 }
 }
]
```

Se você quer desabilitar sons de notificação completamente, você também pode ajustar as configurações de som do seu sistema operacional Linux ou procurar por extensões de controle de notificações no marketplace.

Claro! Vou ajudá-lo a configurar um agente customizado no VS Code.

Para configurar um agente customizado, use o comando **Chat: Configure Custom Agents...** para abrir as configurações de agentes.

```json
[
 {
 "type": "command",
 "details": {
 "key": "workbench.action.chat.configure.customagents"
 }
 }
]
```

Se você deseja criar um novo agente customizado, use o comando **Chat: New Custom Agent...**.

```json
[
 {
 "type": "command",
 "details": {
 "key": "workbench.command.new.agent"
 }
 }
]
```

Além disso, certifique-se de que o modo de agente está habilitado usando a configuração **chat.agent.enabled**:

```json
[
 {
 "type": "setting",
 "details": {
 "key": "chat.agent.enabled",
 "value": "true"
 }
 }
]
```

Se você deseja usar agentes customizados dentro de subagentes, habilite a configuração **chat.customAgentInSubagent.enabled**:

```json
[
 {
 "type": "setting",
 "details": {
 "key": "chat.customAgentInSubagent.enabled",
 "value": "true"
 }
 }
]
```

Ótima pergunta! Eu posso criar agentes customizados para você diretamente aqui no VS Code. Não precisa mudar de "ask" para "agente" — você continua conversando comigo normalmente.

**Como funciona:**

Eu posso:
- ✅ **Criar agentes customizados** com comportamentos específicos
- ✅ **Configurar prompts** e instruções personalizadas
- ✅ **Integrar ferramentas** e comandos
- ✅ **Ajustar o comportamento** do agente conforme necessário

**O que você gostaria que o agente fizesse?**

Por exemplo, você poderia querer um agente para:
- Analisar código de um domínio específico (ex: Azure Cosmos DB, como mencionado nas instruções)
- Gerar documentação
- Executar testes
- Revisar PRs
- Trabalhar com dados específicos do seu projeto

Me conte mais detalhes sobre o que você precisa, e eu configuro o agente para você!

for Ativo Real

This file guides GitHub Copilot on the specific architecture, workflows, and conventions of the **Ativo Real** project.

## 🏗️ Architecture & "Big Picture"

Ativo Real is a cloud-native **GeoPlatform** for land management and topography, designed for **Azure**.

* **Architecture Style**: Azure Native Serverless.
* **Frontend**: React (Vite) + OpenLayers (Maps) hosted on **Azure Static Web Apps**.
* **Backend**: Python (**Azure Functions v2**) acting as an API.
* **Database**: PostgreSQL with **PostGIS** extension (Azure Database for PostgreSQL), critical for spatial validation.
* **Infrastructure**: Bicep (Infrastructure as Code).

**Key Design Principle**: 
* **Frontend is for visualization/input**: Use `OpenLayers` to capture geometries.
* **Backend is for truth/validation**: All complex geometric math (overlaps, intersections) happens in Python using `Shapely` and `GeoAlchemy2`.
* **Strict Topology**: The database enforces validity via `ST_IsValid` constraints.

## 📂 Project Structure & Key Files

The project is monorepo-like inside `novo-projeto/`:

* **Frontend** (`novo-projeto/ativo-real/`):
 * `src/components/MapEditor.tsx`: Main map component using OpenLayers.
 * `src/`: React components (Tailwind CSS).
 * `staticwebapp.config.json`: Azure SWA routing configuration.
* **Backend** (`novo-projeto/backend/`):
 * `function_app.py`: Azure Functions entry point (Routes defined here).
 * `logic_services.py`: **CRITICAL**. Contains pure business logic and geometric validation. Keep this decoupled from HTTP layers.
 * `models.py`: SQLAlchemy ORM definitions.
 * `schemas.py`: Pydantic models for request/response validation.
* **Database** (`novo-projeto/database/`):
 * `init/01_schema.sql`: Source of truth for database schema. **Do not rely on ORM auto-creation**, use SQL scripts.

## 💻 Development Workflow

* **Run Locally**: Use Azure Static Web Apps CLI.
 * Command: `swa start frontend --api-location backend` (Run from `novo-projeto/` root or appropriate context).
 * URLs: Frontend `http://localhost:4280`, Backend `http://localhost:7071`.
* **Database**: Requires local PostgreSQL + PostGIS.
 * Environment Variable: Ensure `DATABASE_URL` is set in `local.settings.json` or shell.
* **Dependencies**:
 * Frontend: `npm install` in `novo-projeto/ativo-real`.
 * Backend: `pip install -r requirements.txt` in `novo-projeto/backend`.

## 📝 Coding Conventions

* **Python (Backend)**:
 * **Validation**: Use `pydantic` heavily for data validation.
 * **ORM**: Use `SQLAlchemy` 2.0+ style.
 * **Geo**: Use `shapely` for geometric operations and `geoalchemy2` for DB interactions.
 * **Session Management**: Use `SessionLocal` pattern. ensuring `db.close()` in `finally` blocks (or context managers).
* **TypeScript (Frontend)**:
 * **Maps**: Prefer `ol` (OpenLayers) for map interactions.
 * **Styling**: Use Tailwind CSS utility classes.
 * **State**: React Hooks.

## 🔗 Integrations

* **MCP Servers**: Located in `novo-projeto/mcp-servers/`.
* **Payments**: InfinitePay integration (referenced in documentation).

## ⚠️ Common Pitfalls

* **ORM vs SQL**: We use SQL

# Copilot Instructions for Ativo Real

Guides AI coding agents on the architecture, workflows, and MVP constraints for this **GeoPlatform** project.

## 🛑 CRITICAL CONSTRAINTS (Read First!)

1. **MVP SCOPE IS FROZEN** (Jan 31, 2026): See `ESCOPO_BLOQUEADO_MVP.md` in workspace. **Do NOT add:**
 - Chat AI / LLM / OpenRouter endpoints
 - Gov APIs (SIGEF, CAR, FUNAI)
 - Advanced exports (CAD/DWG), PWA, WebRTC, image processing
 - Any feature not explicitly listed in MVP allowlist

2. **Cloud-First, Not Local**: Assume Azure deployment for testing. Do NOT suggest `swa start` or local testing as primary workflow.

3. **Preserve Existing Pages**: Pages in `src/pages/` (Login, Dashboards, etc.) are production dependencies. Refactor conservatively; preserve routing unless explicitly asked to remove.

## 🏗️ Architecture: Azure Serverless GeoPlatform

**Stack**: Azure Static Web Apps (Frontend) + Azure Functions (Backend) + PostgreSQL+PostGIS (Database)

**Design Principle**: 
- Frontend = Canvas for visualization & geometry capture (OpenLayers)
- Backend = Truth Engine for math (Shapely, PostGIS validation)
- Database = Strict topology enforcement (ST_IsValid constraints)

**Key Separation**:
- `backend/function_app.py` = HTTP routing only (no logic)
- `backend/logic_services.py` = Pure business logic (geometry, overlaps, validation)
- `backend/models.py` = SQLAlchemy ORM (SQL scripts define schema)

## 📂 Project Layout

``````
novo-projeto/
├── ativo-real/ # React + Vite frontend
│ ├── src/components/ # UI components (Tailwind)
│ ├── src/pages/ # DO NOT DELETE (Login, TopographerDashboard, etc.)
│ ├── src/services/ # API clients
│ └── staticwebapp.config.json # SWA routes
├── backend/ # Python Azure Functions
│ ├── function_app.py # HTTP endpoints (@app.route)
│ ├── logic_services.py # Geometric validation logic
│ ├── models.py # SQLAlchemy ORM
│ ├── schemas.py # Pydantic validators
│ ├── auth_middleware.py # JWT token handling
│ └── requirements.txt # Keep updated for Azure
├── database/
│ └── init/01_schema.sql # Source of truth (not ORM auto-create)
└── mcp-servers/ # Custom MCP integrations
``````

## 💻 Workflows & Commands

**Build Backend**: `cd novo-projeto/backend && pip install -r requirements.txt`

**Deploy to Azure**: Push to GitHub → SWA auto-builds (`App Location: ativo-real`, `API Location: backend`)

**Environment Variables**: Store `DATABASE_URL` in Azure Portal Application Settings, not in code.

## 🔐 Auth & Roles

**JWT Flow** (in `auth_middleware.py`):
- `create_access_token(user_id, role)` → 30 min expiry
- `@require_auth` decorator for protected routes
- `@require_role(['topographer'])` for role-based access

**Roles**: `topographer` (admin) | `client` (limited)

## 🗺️ Spatial Validation Pattern

**All geometric operations follow this flow**:
1. Frontend: Draw in OpenLayers → export as WKT
2. Backend: `logic_services.check_overlap_warnings(wkt, projeto_id, db)` 
3. Database: `ST_Intersects()`, `ST_Within()`, `ST_IsValid()` PostGIS functions
4. Response: Return warnings (non-blocking) or errors (blocking)

**Critical Constants** (in `logic_services.py`):
- `TOLERANCIA_SOBREPOSICAO_GRAUS = 0.0000001` (real overlap detection)
- `TOLERANCIA_SNAP_METROS = 0.5` (snap warning threshold)
- `SRID_OFICIAL = 4674` (SIRGAS 2000 CRS)

## 📋 Data Model (Must Match `01_schema.sql`)

- `users` (id, email, password_hash, role)
- `projects` (id, topographer_id, status)
- `project_clients` (id, project_id, name, cpf, email, phone, address)
- `lots` (id, project_id, client_id, geometry, area, perimeter)
- `wms_layers` (id, project_id, name, url, visible, opacity)
- `neighbors` (id, name, cpf_cnpj, geometry, source)
- `payments` (id, project_id, amount, status, infinitepay_id)
- `chat_messages` (id, project_id, sender_id, message, created_at)

## 🧪 Testing Pattern

**Backend**: Use `SessionLocal` with `try/finally` for DB cleanup.
``````python
db = SessionLocal()
try:
 result = logic_services.check_overlap_warnings(wkt, proyecto_id, db)
finally:
 db.close()
``````

**Frontend**: Mock API responses in `src/mocks/` for component testing.

## 🚀 Integrations

- **InfinitePay**: POST `/api/payments/infinitepay` (webhook handler)
- **WMS Layers**: User-provided URLs (no automatic fetching)
- **OpenLayers**: Always use `ol.Map`, `ol.layer.Tile/Vector`, `ol.interaction.Draw`

## ⚠️ Anti-Patterns

- ❌ ORM auto-creation (`metadata.create_all()`) → use SQL scripts
- ❌ Global state (Redux/Zustand) → use React Context + Hooks
- ❌ Heavy UI libs (MUI/Ant) → stick with Tailwind + basic HTML
- ❌ Complex st
```

version: '3.8'

services:
 # 👴 Agente 1: Banco de Dados Espacial
 db:
 image: postgis/postgis:15-3.3
 container_name: ativoreal_db
 environment:
 POSTGRES_USER: admin
 POSTGRES_PASSWORD: senha_segura_dev
 POSTGRES_DB: ativoreal_geo
 volumes:
 - ./database/init:/docker-entrypoint-initdb.d
 - postgres_data:/var/lib/postgresql/data
 ports:
 - "5432:5432"
 healthcheck:
 test: ["CMD-SHELL", "pg_isready -U admin -d ativoreal_geo"]
 interval: 5s
 timeout: 5s
 retries: 5

 # 🐍 Agente 2: Backend (Será implementado na próxima fase)
 backend:
 build: ./backend
 container_name: ativoreal_api
 volumes:
 - ./backend:/app
 ports:
 - "8000:8000"
 depends_on:
 db:
 condition: service_healthy
 environment:
 DATABASE_URL: postgresql://admin:senha_segura_dev@db:5432/ativoreal_geo

volumes:
 postgres_data:

# 🏗️ Especificação de Arquitetura: Bem Real SaaS

Este documento define a lógica, fluxo de dados e requisitos para o sistema de regularização fundiária, focado na segregação de 3 perfis de usuários.

## 🎭 Os 3 Atores (Perfis)

### 1. 👷‍♂️ O Topógrafo (Admin/Orquestrador)
* **Papel:** Criador do Projeto e Revisor Técnico.
* **Responsabilidades:**
 * Define o perímetro global da Fazenda (Área Mãe).
 * Configura as regras do desmembramento (ex: tamanho mínimo de lote).
 * Gera os "Links Mágicos" para convidar os proprietários.
 * **Visão de Deus (God Mode):** Vê o mapa completo com todos os lotes (preenchidos e vazios) e pode corrigir desenhos dos clientes.

### 2. 🤠 O Proprietário Individual (Cliente Final)
* **Papel:** Usuário Leigo focado em regularizar sua terra isolada.
* **Fluxo:**
 * Entra via Link Único (sem cadastro de senha).
 * Desenha APENAS sua área.
 * Paga pelo serviço (Checkout).
 * Assina contrato digitalmente.

### 3. 🏘️ O Condômino/Vizinho (Cliente Multi)
* **Papel:** Parte de um quebra-cabeça maior (Desmembramento).
* **Diferencial:**
 * Ao entrar no mapa, ele **VÊ** os limites dos vizinhos que já desenharam (camada cinza, bloqueada).
 * O sistema força o "Snap" (imã) para conectar sua cerca na cerca do vizinho, evitando buracos.

---

## 📐 Fluxo de Dados e User Stories

### User Story 1: O Fluxo do Topógrafo (Setup)
1. **Criação do Projeto:**
 * Topógrafo faz upload de um KML/Shapefile da "Fazenda Mãe" ou desenha no mapa.
 * Sistema valida se essa área mãe sobrepõe terra indígena ou quilombola (SIGEF Check).
2. **Disparo de Convites:**
 * Input: Lista de emails/phones dos clientes.
 * Processo: Sistema gera tokens JWT únicos com validade (ex: 7 dias) contendo `projeto_id` e permissão `draw:self`.
 * Output: Envio de WhatsApp/Email automático.

### User Story 2: O Fluxo do Cliente (Execution)
1. **Acesso Seguro:**
 * Cliente clica no link -> API valida token -> Frontend carrega o mapa centralizado na região do projeto.
2. **Desenho Guiado (The Pit of Success):**
 * O cliente usa ferramenta de polígono.
 * **Restrição Hard:** O desenho não pode sair de dentro do polígono da "Fazenda Mãe" definido pelo Topógrafo.
 * **Validação Live:** Ao fechar o polígono, o Backend verifica `ST_Intersects` com vizinhos e SIGEF.
 * *Sucesso:* Área fica verde. Botão "Avançar" habilita.
 * *Erro:* Área fica vermelha e mostra mensagem amigável: "Sua cerca está invadindo a área do Sr. João. Por favor, ajuste os pontos."
3. **Checkout e Legal:**
 * Tela de Pagamento (Stripe/InfinitePay).
 * Geração de Contrato PDF com os dados do desenho (Área, Perímetro, Confrontantes).

---

## 🛠️ Stack Tecnológica Sugerida (Geometria & Dados)

### Backend (The Brain)
* **Linguagem:** Python 3.11 (Azure Functions).
* **Libs Geométricas:** 
 * `Shapely`: Para operações booleanas (Union, Difference, Intersection) e validação topológica (`is_valid`).
 * `PyProj`: Para cálculo preciso de área geodésica (m² reais na curvatura da terra).
* **Banco de Dados:** PostgreSQL + **PostGIS** (Mandatório).
 * Tipos: `GEOGRAPHY` para cálculos de área/distância, `GEOMETRY` (SIRGAS 2000) para operações de desenho.

### Frontend (The Canvas)
* **Framework:** React + Vite.
* **Map Engine:** **OpenLayers**.
 * *Por que não Google Maps?* Google Maps é ruim para edição precisa de polígonos. OpenLayers permite Snapping (imã) nativo e manipulação de vértices profissional.
* **State Management:** Zustand ou Context API (para guardar o estado do desenho antes de salvar).

---

## 🔒 Modelo de Segurança (Nível de Dados)

| Ator | Insert | Update | Delete | Select (Ver) |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Topógrafo** | ✅ Projetos, Lotes | ✅ Tudo | ✅ Tudo | ✅ Tudo |
| **Cliente** | ✅ Seu Lote (Rascunho) | ✅ Seu Lote (Até Pagar) | ❌ | ✅ Seu Lote + Vizinhos (Read-only) |

### Regra de Ouro da Topologia
> "A soma das partes não pode ser maior que o todo, e as partes não podem se sobrepor."

1. **Constraint de Banco:** `Check(ST_Within(lote.geom, projeto.geom))`
2. **Constraint de Banco:** `Exclude(lote.geom WITH &&)` (Evita sobreposição no nível do índice espacial).

---

## 📝 Próximos Passos (Implementação)

1. Criar tabela `Usuarios` com perfis (`ROLE_TOPOGRAPHER`, `ROLE_CLIENT`).
2. Criar rota `POST /invite` que gera links assinados.
3. Implementar o middleware que impede o Cliente de salvar se o desenho não passar na validação geométrica do Backend.

import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import Map from 'ol/Map';
import View from 'ol/View';
import TileLayer from 'ol/layer/Tile';
import OSM from 'ol/source/OSM';
import VectorLayer from 'ol/layer/Vector';
import VectorSource from 'ol/source/Vector';
import { Draw, Snap, Modify } from 'ol/interaction';
import { fromLonLat, toLonLat } from 'ol/proj';
import axios from 'axios';

// 🎨 Agente 3: Especialista em Mapas
// Responsabilidade: Capturar coordenadas e feedback visual. NENHUMA lógica complexa aqui.

const MapEditor = () => {
 const mapRef = useRef(null);
 const [mapObj, setMapObj] = useState(null);
 const [status, setStatus] = useState('Pronto para desenhar');

 useEffect(() => {
 if (mapRef.current && !mapObj) {
 // 1. Inicialização do Mapa
 const source = new VectorSource();
 const vector = new VectorLayer({
 source: source,
 style: {
 'fill-color': 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.2)',
 'stroke-color': '#ffcc33',
 'stroke-width': 2,
 'circle-radius': 7,
 'circle-fill-color': '#ffcc33',
 },
 });

 const map = new Map({
 target: mapRef.current,
 layers: [
 new TileLayer({
 source: new OSM(), // Mapa base (OpenStreetMap)
 }),
 vector,
 ],
 view: new View({
 center: fromLonLat([-47.9292, -15.7801]), // Brasília
 zoom: 12, // Zoom inicial
 }),
 });

 // 2. Ferramentas de Interação
 
 // DRAW: Permite desenhar polígonos
 const draw = new Draw({
 source: source,
 type: 'Polygon',
 });

 // SNAP: Imã magnético para garantir precisão topológica (conecta vértices exatos)
 const snap = new Snap({ source: source });

 map.addInteraction(draw);
 map.addInteraction(snap);

 // 3. Evento: Ao terminar o desenho
 draw.on('drawend', async (event) => {
 const feature = event.feature;
 const geometry = feature.getGeometry();
 
 // Coletar coordenadas em EPSG:3857 (Web Mercator)
 const coords3857 = geometry.getCoordinates()[0]; // Outer ring
 
 // Converter para EPSG:4326 (Lat/Lon) para enviar ao backend
 const coords4326 = coords3857.map(coord => {
 const lonlat = toLonLat(coord);
 return [lonlat[1], lonlat[0]]; // [Lat, Lon] formato esperado pelo Python Schema
 });

 setStatus('Validando com Agente Backend...');

 try {
 // 4. Delegação: Envia para a API validar a Regra de Negócio
 const payload = {
 matricula: `MAT-${Date.now()}`, // Gerando ID temporário
 proprietario: "Usuário Teste",
 coordinates: coords4326
 };

 const response = await axios.post('/api/lotes/', payload);
 
 setStatus(`✅ Lote aprovado! ID: ${response.data.id} - Área: ${response.data.area_ha} ha`);
 console.log("Sucesso:", response.data);
 
 // Opcional: Mudar cor para verde para indicar sucesso (necessita style function, simplificando aqui)
 
 } catch (error) {
 console.error("Erro na validação:", error);
 
 // 5. Feedback de Erro de Regra de Negócio (Sobreposição)
 if (error.response && error.response.status === 400) {
 const errorMsg = error.response.data.detail || "Erro de validação";
 alert(`⛔ BLOQUEADO PELO BACKEND:\n\n${errorMsg}`);
 setStatus(`❌ Rejeitado: ${errorMsg}`);
 
 // Remove o desenho inválido do mapa
 source.removeFeature(feature);
 } else {
 alert('Erro de conexão com o servidor.');
 }
 }
 });

 setMapObj(map);
 }
 }, [mapRef, mapObj]);

 return (
 <div style={{ position: 'relative', width: '100%', height: '100%' }}>
 <div 
 ref={mapRef} 
 style={{ width: '100%', height: '100%', cursor: 'crosshair' }} 
 />
 <div style={{
 position: 'absolute',
 bottom: 20,
 left: 20,
 background: 'white',
 padding: '10px 20px',
 borderRadius: '8px',
 boxShadow: '0 4px 6px rgba(0,0,0,0.1)',
 zIndex: 100,
 fontWeight: 'bold',
 color: '#333'
 }}>
 Status: {status}
 </div>
 </div>
 );
};

export default MapEditor;

import React from 'react';
import './TopoIcon.css';

/**
 * Componente para ícones topográficos BEM REAL
 * Suporta coloração dinâmica e controle de tamanho
 * 
 * @param Icon - Componente SVG (Lucide ou similar)
 * @param size - Tamanho em pixels (default 24)
 * @param isActive - Estado ativo/inativo (bronze/cinza)
 * @param disabled - Estado desabilitado
 * @param color - Cor customizada (sobrescreve isActive)
 * @param className - Classes CSS adicionais
 */
const TopoIcon = ({
 Icon,
 size = 24,
 isActive = true,
 disabled = false,
 color,
 className = '',
 ariaLabel,
}) => {
 // Cores do Design System BEM REAL
 const BRONZE_ACTIVE = '#CD7F32'; // Bronze Fosco - Ativo
 const GRAY_INACTIVE = '#A0A0A0'; // Cinza - Inativo
 const DARK_DISABLED = '#4A4A4A'; // Cinza Escuro - Desabilitado

 const getIconColor = () => {
 if (color) return color;
 if (disabled) return DARK_DISABLED;
 return isActive ? BRONZE_ACTIVE : GRAY_INACTIVE;
 };

 const iconColor = getIconColor();

 if (!Icon) return null;

 return (
 <Icon
 width={size}
 height={size}
 style={{
 stroke: iconColor,
 fill: 'none',
 transition: 'stroke 0.2s ease-in-out',
 marginRight: '8px',
 verticalAlign: 'middle',
 opacity: disabled ? 0.5 : 1,
 cursor: disabled ? 'not-allowed' : 'inherit',
 }}
 className={`topo-icon ${className} ${disabled ? 'disabled' : ''}`}
 aria-label={ariaLabel}
 aria-disabled={disabled}
 role="img"
 />
 );
};

export default TopoIcon;

from sqlalchemy import Column, Integer, String, Numeric, TIMESTAMP, text, ForeignKey, Enum, JSON, Boolean
from sqlalchemy.orm import relationship
from sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import UUID
from geoalchemy2 import Geometry
import uuid
import enum
from database import Base

class SigefIncra(Base):
 __tablename__ = "sigef_incra"
 # Tabela de referencia oficial (Read-only para a aplicacao)
 id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True)
 codigo_imovel = Column(String(50))
 detentor = Column(String(200))
 geom = Column(Geometry("POLYGON", srid=4674))

class Projeto(Base):
 __tablename__ = "projetos"
 
 id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
 nome = Column(String(100), nullable=False)
 descricao = Column(String, nullable=True)
 tipo = Column(String(50), default='INDIVIDUAL') # Enum no banco, string aqui por simplicidade
 status = Column(String(50), default='RASCUNHO')
 
 # REMOVIDO: Geometria e Matricula da Mae (Simplificação)
 
 criado_em = Column(TIMESTAMP, server_default=text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP"))
 atualizado_em = Column(TIMESTAMP, server_default=text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP"), onupdate=text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP"))
 
 # Relacao com Lotes
 lotes = relationship("Lote", back_populates="projeto", cascade="all, delete-orphan")

class Lote(Base):
 __tablename__ = "lotes"

 id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
 projeto_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("projetos.id"), nullable=True)
 
 # Dados Cliente
 nome_cliente = Column(String(150))
 email_cliente = Column(String(150))
 telefone_cliente = Column(String(20))
 cpf_cnpj_cliente = Column(String(20))
 
 # Acesso
 token_acesso = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), default=uuid.uuid4, index=True)
 link_expira_em = Column(TIMESTAMP, nullable=True)

 # Tecnico
 matricula = Column(String(50))
 geom = Column(Geometry("POLYGON", srid=4674))
 area_ha = Column(Numeric(10, 4))
 perimetro_m = Column(Numeric(10, 2))
 
 # Workflow
 status = Column(String(50), default='PENDENTE')
 metadata_validacao = Column(JSON, nullable=True) # Logs de sobreposicao etc
 contrato_url = Column(String, nullable=True)
 
 criado_em = Column(TIMESTAMP, server_default=text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP"))
 atualizado_em = Column(TIMESTAMP, server_default=text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP"), onupdate=text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP"))
 
 projeto = relationship("Projeto", back_populates="lotes")
 pagamentos = relationship("Pagamento", back_populates="lote")

 @property
 def proprietario(self):
 return self.nome_cliente

 @property
 def warnings(self):
 if self.metadata_validacao and isinstance(self.metadata_validacao, dict):
 return self.metadata_validacao.get("warnings", [])
 return []

class Pagamento(Base):
 __tablename__ = "pagamentos"
 
 id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
 lote_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("lotes.id"))
 valor_total = Column(Numeric(10, 2), nullable=False)
 valor_pago = Column(Numeric(10, 2), default=0)
 status = Column(String(20), default='PENDENTE')
 gateway_id = Column(String(100))
 data_pagamento = Column(TIMESTAMP)
 
 lote = relationship("Lote", back_populates="pagamentos")

# ==================== MODELOS PAY AS YOU GO ====================

class StatusAssinaturaEnum(enum.Enum):
 """Enum para status de assinatura"""
 TRIAL = "TRIAL"
 PENDENTE = "PENDENTE"
 ATIVA = "ATIVA"
 CANCELADA = "CANCELADA"
 SUSPENSA = "SUSPENSA"
 EXPIRADA = "EXPIRADA"

class PlanoPagamento(Base):
 """Planos de assinatura disponíveis no sistema"""
 __tablename__ = "planos_pagamento"
 
 id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
 nome = Column(String(50), unique=True, nullable=False)
 descricao = Column(String)
 preco_mensal = Column(Numeric(10, 2), nullable=False, default=0)
 
 # Limites de Uso
 max_projetos = Column(Integer, default=-1) # -1 = ilimitado
 max_lotes_por_projeto = Column(Integer, default=-1)
 storage_mb = Column(Integer, default=100)
 
 # Recursos/Features
 permite_export_kml = Column(Boolean, default=False)
 permite_export_shp = Column(Boolean, default=False)
 permite_export_dxf = Column(Boolean, default=False)
 permite_api_access = Column(Boolean, default=False)
 
 # Metadata flexível
 features = Column(JSON)
 
 # Controle
 ativo = Column(Boolean, default=True)
 ordem_exibicao = Column(Integer, default=0)
 
 # Timestamps
 criado_em = Column(TIMESTAMP, server_default=text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP"))
 atualizado_em = Column(TIMESTAMP, server_default=text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP"), onupdate=text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP"))
 
 # Relationships
 assinaturas = relationship("Assinatura", back_populates="plano")

class Assinatura(Base):
 """Assinaturas dos usuários aos planos"""
 __tablename__ = "assinaturas"
 
 id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
 usuario_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("lotes.id"), index=True) # Vincula ao cliente/topógrafo
 plano_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("planos_pagamento.id"))
 
 # Status e Ciclo de Vida
 status = Column(Enum(StatusAssinaturaEnum), default=StatusAssinaturaEnum.TRIAL)
 
 # Datas Importantes
 inicio_em = Column(TIMESTAMP, server_default=text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP"))
 expira_em = Column(TIMESTAMP)
 cancelada_em = Column(TIMESTAMP)
 suspensa_em = Column(TIMESTAMP)
 proximo_pagamento = Column(TIMESTAMP)
 
 # Integração com Gateway de Pagamento
 gateway_subscription_id = Column(String(100), index=True)
 gateway_customer_id = Column(String(100))
 metodo_pagamento = Column(String(20)) # PIX, CARTAO, BOLETO
 
 # Histórico e Auditoria
 metadata = Column(JSON)
 tentativas_cobranca = Column(Integer, default=0)
 ultima_tentativa_cobranca = Column(TIMESTAMP)
 
 # Timestamps
 criado_em = Column(TIMESTAMP, server_default=text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP"))
 atualizado_em = Column(TIMESTAMP, server_default=text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP"), onupdate=text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP"))
 
 # Relationships
 plano = relationship("PlanoPagamento", back_populates="assinaturas")
 historico = relationship("HistoricoAssinatura", back_populates="assinatura", cascade="all, delete-orphan")

class HistoricoAssinatura(Base):
 """Log de todas as alterações em assinaturas"""
 __tablename__ = "historico_assinaturas"
 
 id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
 assinatura_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("assinaturas.id"), index=True)
 
 # Ação Realizada
 acao = Column(String(50), nullable=False) # CRIADA, RENOVADA, UPGRADE, DOWNGRADE, CANCELADA, SUSPENSA
 
 # Mudança de Plano (se aplicável)
 plano_anterior_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("planos_pagamento.id"))
 plano_novo_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("planos_pagamento.id"))
 
 # Valores
 valor_pago = Column(Numeric(10, 2))
 
 # Detalhes Adicionais
 detalhes = Column(JSON)
 
 # Auditoria
 criado_em = Column(TIMESTAMP, server_default=text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP"))
 criado_por = Column(String(100)) # Email/ID do usuário que fez a ação
 
 # Relationships
 assinatura = relationship("Assinatura", back_populates="historico")

from pydantic import BaseModel, validator
from typing import List, Tuple, Optional, Any, Dict
from datetime import datetime
from uuid import UUID
from enum import Enum

class LoteBase(BaseModel):
 matricula: str
 proprietario: str
 projeto_id: Optional[int] = None

class LoteCreate(LoteBase):
 coordinates: List[Tuple[float, float]] 

 @validator("coordinates")
 def validate_polygon_closure(cls, v):
 if len(v) < 3:
 raise ValueError("Um poligono precisa de pelo menos 3 pontos")
 if v[0] != v[-1]:
 v.append(v[0])
 return v

class LoteResponse(LoteBase):
 id: int
 area_ha: Optional[float] = None
 perimetro_m: Optional[float] = None
 warnings: List[str] = []
 metadata_validacao: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None
 token_acesso: Optional[UUID] = None
 criado_em: Optional[datetime]
 
 class Config:
 from_attributes = True

class ProjetoBase(BaseModel):
 nome: str
 descricao: Optional[str] = None
 tipo: Optional[str] = "INDIVIDUAL"

class ProjetoCreate(ProjetoBase):
 coordinates: List[Tuple[float, float]] = [] 

 @validator("coordinates")
 def validate_polygon_closure(cls, v):
 if not v:
 return v
 if len(v) < 3:
 raise ValueError("Um poligono precisa de pelo menos 3 pontos")
 if v[0] != v[-1]:
 v.append(v[0])
 return v

class ProjetoResponse(ProjetoBase):
 id: int
 criado_em: Optional[datetime]
 
 class Config:
 from_attributes = True

# ==================== SCHEMAS PAY AS YOU GO ====================

class StatusAssinatura(str, Enum):
 """Enum para status de assinatura"""
 TRIAL = "TRIAL"
 PENDENTE = "PENDENTE"
 ATIVA = "ATIVA"
 CANCELADA = "CANCELADA"
 SUSPENSA = "SUSPENSA"
 EXPIRADA = "EXPIRADA"

class PlanoBase(BaseModel):
 """Schema base para Plano de Pagamento"""
 nome: str
 descricao: Optional[str] = None
 preco_mensal: float
 max_projetos: int = -1
 max_lotes_por_projeto: int = -1
 storage_mb: int = 100
 permite_export_kml: bool = False
 permite_export_shp: bool = False
 permite_export_dxf: bool = False
 permite_api_access: bool = False

class PlanoResponse(PlanoBase):
 """Schema de resposta para Plano de Pagamento"""
 id: int
 features: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None
 ativo: bool = True
 ordem_exibicao: int = 0
 criado_em: Optional[datetime] = None
 
 class Config:
 from_attributes = True

class AssinaturaCreate(BaseModel):
 """Schema para criar assinatura"""
 plano_id: int
 usuario_id: int
 metodo_pagamento: Optional[str] = None # PIX, CARTAO, BOLETO

class AssinaturaResponse(BaseModel):
 """Schema de resposta para Assinatura"""
 id: int
 usuario_id: int
 plano_id: int
 status: StatusAssinatura
 inicio_em: datetime
 expira_em:
```

# Copilot Instructions for Ativo Real

This file guides GitHub Copilot on the specific architecture, workflows, and conventions of the **Ativo Real** project.

## 🏗️ Architecture & "Big Picture"

Ativo Real is a cloud-native **GeoPlatform** for land management and topography, designed for **Azure**.

* **Architecture Style**: Azure Native Serverless.
* **Frontend**: React (Vite) + OpenLayers (Maps) hosted on **Azure Static Web Apps**.
* **Backend**: Python (**Azure Functions v2**) acting as an API.
* **Database**: PostgreSQL with **PostGIS** extension (Azure Database for PostgreSQL), critical for spatial validation.
* **Infrastructure**: Bicep (Infrastructure as Code).

**Key Design Principle**: 
* **Frontend is for visualization/input**: Use `OpenLayers` to capture geometries.
* **Backend is for truth/validation**: All complex geometric math (overlaps, intersections) happens in Python using `Shapely` and `GeoAlchemy2`.
* **Strict Topology**: The database enforces validity via `ST_IsValid` constraints.

## 📂 Project Structure & Key Files

The project is monorepo-like inside `novo-projeto/`:

* **Frontend** (`novo-projeto/ativo-real/`):
 * `src/components/MapEditor.tsx`: Main map component using OpenLayers.
 * `src/`: React components (Tailwind CSS).
 * `staticwebapp.config.json`: Azure SWA routing configuration.
* **Backend** (`novo-projeto/backend/`):
 * `function_app.py`: Azure Functions entry point (Routes defined here).
 * `logic_services.py`: **CRITICAL**. Contains pure business logic and geometric validation. Keep this decoupled from HTTP layers.
 * `models.py`: SQLAlchemy ORM definitions.
 * `schemas.py`: Pydantic models for request/response validation.
* **Database** (`novo-projeto/database/`):
 * `init/01_schema.sql`: Source of truth for database schema. **Do not rely on ORM auto-creation**, use SQL scripts.

## 💻 Development Workflow

* **Run Locally**: Use Azure Static Web Apps CLI.
 * Command: `swa start frontend --api-location backend` (Run from `novo-projeto/` root or appropriate context).
 * URLs: Frontend `http://localhost:4280`, Backend `http://localhost:7071`.
* **Database**: Requires local PostgreSQL + PostGIS.
 * Environment Variable: Ensure `DATABASE_URL` is set in `local.settings.json` or shell.
* **Dependencies**:
 * Frontend: `npm install` in `novo-projeto/ativo-real`.
 * Backend: `pip install -r requirements.txt` in `novo-projeto/backend`.

## 📝 Coding Conventions

* **Python (Backend)**:
 * **Validation**: Use `pydantic` heavily for data validation.
 * **ORM**: Use `SQLAlchemy` 2.0+ style.
 * **Geo**: Use `shapely` for geometric operations and `geoalchemy2` for DB interactions.
 * **Session Management**: Use `SessionLocal` pattern. ensuring `db.close()` in `finally` blocks (or context managers).
* **TypeScript (Frontend)**:
 * **Maps**: Prefer `ol` (OpenLayers) for map interactions.
 * **Styling**: Use Tailwind CSS utility classes.
 * **State**: React Hooks.

## 🔗 Integrations

* **MCP Servers**: Located in `novo-projeto/mcp-servers/`.
* **Payments**: InfinitePay integration (referenced in documentation).

## ⚠️ Common Pitfalls

* **ORM vs SQL**: We use SQL scripts (`database/init/`) for schema definition, not `metadata.create_all()`.
* **GeoJSON Handling**: Ensure standard GeoJSON format when passing data between Front/Back.
* **Snap**: Frontend map editor implements snapping to ensuring topology consistency before sending to backend.
